[OUTPUT_HEAD]
     1	Testem Configuration File
     2	=========================
     3	
     4	This document will go into more detail about the Testem configuration file and list in glorious detail all of its available options. The config file is in either JSON format or YAML format and can be called any of the following
     5	
     6	* `testem.json`
     7	* `.testem.json`
     8	* `testem.yml`
     9	* `.testem.yml`
    10	* `testem.js`
    11	* `.testem.js`
    12	* `testem.cjs`
    13	* `.testem.cjs`
    14	
    15	The file is looked for in the user's current directory.
    16	
    17	An Example
    18	----------
    19	
    20	Here's an example `testem.json` file
    21	
    22	    {
    23	        "framework": "mocha",
    24	        "src_files": [
    25	            "src/*.js",
    26	            "tests/*_tests.js"
    27	        ]
    28	    }
    29	
    30	Here's an example `testem.js` file that defines a [custom reporter](custom_reporter.md):
    31	
    32	    var CustomReporter = require('./my-custom-reporter');
    33	    module.exports = {
    34	        "framework": "mocha",
    35	        "src_files": [
    36	            "src/*.js",
    37	            "tests/*_tests.js"
    38	        ]
    39	        "reporter": new CustomReporter()
    40	    };
    41	
    42	
    43	Common Configuration Options
    44	----------------------------
    45	
    46	* **framework** - the test framework that you are using, in the browser, in the case that you are not also using the `test_page` option. The possible values at the moment are `jasmine`, `jasmine2`, `qunit`, `mocha`, `custom`, and `tap`. Defaults to `jasmine`.
    47	* **src_files** - the location of your source files. This should be the code that you author directly, and not generated source files. So, if you are writing in CoffeeScript or TypeScript, this should be your `.coffee` or `.ts` files. If you are writing in Javascript, this would just be your `.js` files, but if you have a compile step for your JS, this would be the `.js` file pre-compilation. The files matched here are what Testem watches for modification (the *watch list*) so that it promptly re-runs the tests when any of them are saved.
    48	* **serve_files** - the location of the source files to be served to the browser. If don't have a compilation step, don't set this option, and it will default to *src_files*. If you have a compilation step, you should set this to the `*.js` file(s) that result from the compilation.
    49	* **test_page** - if you want to use a custom test page to run your tests, put its path here. In most cases, when you use this option, the *src_files* option becomes unnecessary because Testem simply adds all requested files into the watch list. You will also make sure that you include the `/testem.js` script in your test page if you use this option - simply include it with a script tag just below the include for your test framework, i.e. `jasmine.js`.
    50	* **launchers** - this option allows you to set up custom process launchers which can be used to run Node programs and indeed any kind of process within Testem.
    51	
    52	## Option Reference
    53	
    54	### Potentially available browsers
    55	
    56	Chrome, Chrome Canary, Chromium, Firefox, IE, Opera, PhantomJS, Safari, Safari Technology Preview
    57	
    58	### CLI-level options:
    59	
    60	    file:                    [String]  configuration file (testem.json, .testem.json, testem.yml, .testem.yml)
    61	    host:                    [String]  server host to use (localhost)
    62	    port:                    [Number]  server port to use (7357)
    63	    launch:                  [Array]   Comma-delimited list of launchers to use for current runs (defaults to current mode)
    64	    skip:                    [Array]   Comma-delimited list of launchers to skip
    65	    debug:                   [Boolean] debug mode (false)
    66	    test_page:               [String]  string (or array of string) paths to the pages to use to run tests
    67	    growl:                   [Boolean] enables growl / native notifications (false)
    68	    bail_on_uncaught_error:  [Boolean] whether process should exit with error status when there are top level uncaught errors (via `window.onerror`) (true)
    69	
    70	### Config-level options:
    71	
    72	    custom_browser_socket_events    [Object]  an object containing keys corresponding to event names that point to handler functions, which are to be added to the browser socket
    73	    browser_disconnect_timeout   [Number]  timeout to error after disconnect in seconds (10s)
    74	    browser_reconnect_limit      [Number]  number of browser reconnects to allow (3)
    75	    browser_start_timeout        [Number]  timeout to error after browser start in seconds (30s)
    76	    browser_paths:               [Object]  hash of browsers (keys) to an string of their binary paths (values)
    77	    browser_exes:                [Object]  hash of browsers (keys) to an
[OUTPUT_TAIL]
    96	    phantomjs_args:              [Array]   custom arguments for the phantomjs launcher from http://phantomjs.org/api/command-line.html
    97	    phantomjs_launch_script:     [String]  path of custom phantomjs launch script
    98	    proxies                      [Object]  path to options including `onlyContentTypes` and https://github.com/nodejitsu/node-http-proxy#options
    99	    reporter:                    [String]  name of the reporter to be used in ci mode ("tap" (default), "xunit", "dot", "teamcity") or an object implementing https://github.com/testem/testem/blob/master/docs/custom_reporter.md
   100	    report_file:                 [String]  file to write test results to (stdout)
   101	    route or routes:             [Object]  overrides for assets paths
   102	    socket_heartbeat_timeout     [Number]  heartbeat timeout on browser socket in seconds (defaults to `browser_disconnect_timeout` if `browser_disconnect_timeout` is provided. Else, 5s)
   103	    src_files:                   [Array]   string or array list of files or file patterns to use
   104	    src_files_ignore:            [Array]   string or array list of files or file patterns to exclude from usage
   105	    serve_files:                 [Array]   string or array list of files or file patterns to inject into test playground (defaults to `src_files`)
   106	    serve_files_ignore:          [Array]   string or array list of files or file patterns to exclude from test playground (defaults to `src_files_ignore`)
   107	    single_run                   [Boolean] whether or not test is to be single-run
   108	    socket_server_options        [Object]  options to start socketio and engineio within testem's server. Options can be found here: https://socket.io/docs/server-api/
   109	    stdout_stream                [Stream]  the stdout stream to use (defaults to `process.stdout`)
   110	    tap_failed_tests_only        [Boolean] log only failed tests (`not ok`) in TAP reporting
   111	    tap_quiet_logs               [Boolean] whether to suppress non-failing console logs (_not_ pass/fail info) in TAP reporting
   112	    timeout:                     [Number]  timeout for a browser
   113	    unsafe_file_serving:         [Boolean] allow serving directories that are not in your CWD (false)
   114	    url:                         [String]  url server runs at (http://{host}:{port}/)
   115	    user_data_dir:               [String]  directory to initialize the browser user data directories (default a temporary directory)
   116	    watch_files:                 [Array]   string or array list of files or file patterns to watch changes of (defaults to `src_files`)
   117	    xunit_exclude_stack:         [Boolean] whether to exclude stack traces in xunit reporter
   118	    xunit_intermediate_output    [Boolean] print tap output for the xunit reporter (false)
   119	
   120	### HTTPS:
   121	
   122	Testem allows the test page to be served via HTTPS. To enable HTTPS either the pfx or the cert and key options need to be specificed. More details around HTTPS in Node can be found here: https://nodejs.org/dist/latest-v4.x/docs/api/https.html#https_https_createserver_options_requestlistener
   123	
   124	    cert:                        [String]  path to a public x509 certificate to use
   125	    key:                         [String]  path to a private key to use for SS
   126	    pfx:                         [String]  path to certificate, private key and CA certificates to use for SSL
   127	
   128	### Available hooks:
   129	
   130	Hooks can be defined as a string in which case they run as a shell command or as a function in which case they will be passed 3 arguments: the Testem config object, a data object if present (see below), and a callback which should be invoked with a falsey argument (or no arguments) to indicate a passing result or with a truthy argument (such as an `Error` object) to indicate a failing result.
   131	
   132	    on_start:             Runs on suite startup
   133	    on_change:            Runs when a (non-config) file being watched is changed. Has a data object with a `file` property set to the changed file's path
   134	    before_tests:         Runs before every run of tests
   135	    after_tests:          Runs after every run of tests
   136	    on_exit:              Runs before suite exits
   137	
   138	### Returning a Promise from `testem.js`
   139	
   140	If you need to resolve some async values in your configuration you can return a promise from `testem.js`. The resolved value of
   141	the promise will be passed to testem as configuration.
   142	
   143	```javascript
   144	'use strict';
   145	
   146	async function getFramework() {
   147	  //do something async here, like generating a list of launchers based on configuration
   148	  return 'qunit';
   149	}
   150	
   151	module.exports = async function() {
   152	  return {
   153	    framework: await getFramework(),
   154	  }
   155	};
   156	```

    13	function replaceGlobals(newGlobals, originalGlobals) {
    14	  for (let key in newGlobals) {
    15	    originalGlobals[key] = global[key];
    16	    global[key] = newGlobals[key];
    17	  }
    18	}
    19	
    20	function restoreGlobals(originalGlobals) {
    21	  for (let key in originalGlobals) {
    22	    global[key] = originalGlobals[key];
    23	  }
    24	}

   512	        );

'use strict';

function FakeReporter() {
  this.results = [];
  this.total = 0;
  this.pass = 0;
  this.skipped = 0;
}

FakeReporter.prototype.report = function(prefix, result) {
  if (result.passed) {
    this.pass++;
  }
  if (result.skipped) {
    this.skipped++;
  }
  this.total++;
  this.results.push({ result: result });
};
FakeReporter.prototype.finish = function() {};
FakeReporter.prototype.onStart = function() {};
FakeReporter.prototype.onEnd = function() {};
FakeReporter.prototype.reportMetadata = function() {};

module.exports = FakeReporter;
---
'use strict';

var EventEmitter = require('events').EventEmitter;

class FakeServer {
  set() {}
}

/**
 * A mock for a socket from socket.io
 */

function FakeSocket() {
  this.server = new FakeServer();
}

/**
 * Inherits from `EventEmitter`.
 */

FakeSocket.prototype.__proto__ = EventEmitter.prototype;

module.exports = FakeSocket;

cd /app && cat > lib/utils/reporter.js <<'EOF'
'use strict';

const Bluebird = require('bluebird');
const EventEmitter = require('events').EventEmitter;
const log = require('npmlog');

const reporters = require('../reporters');
const isa = require('./isa');
const ReportFile = require('./report-file');

function setupReporter(name, out, config, app) {
  let reporter;

  if (isa(name, String)) {
    let TestReporter = reporters[name];
    if (TestReporter) {
      reporter = new TestReporter(false, out, config, app);
    }
  } else if (isa(name, Function)) {
    // name is a constructor function, ignore new-cap and instantiate
    // eslint-disable-next-line new-cap
    reporter = new name(false, out, config, app);
  } else {
    reporter = name;
  }

  if (!reporter) {
    throw new Error('Test reporter `' + name + '` not found.');
  }

  return reporter;
}


class Reporter extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(app, stdout, path) {
    super();
    this.total = 0;
    this.passed = 0;
    this.skipped = 0;
    this.todo = 0;

    this.bailReason = null;
    this.testsRanBeforeBail = null;
    this.bailLauncher = null;
    this.failuresByLauncher = {};
    this.failedTests = [];
    this.suppressed = 0;

    if (path) {
      this.reportFile = new ReportFile(path);
    }

    let config = app.config;

    this.bailThreshold = this.getBailThreshold(config);

    if (path && config.get('xunit_intermediate_output') && config.get('reporter') === 'xunit') {
      this.reporters = [
        setupReporter('tap', stdout, config, app),
        setupReporter(config.get('reporter'), this.reportFile.outputStream, config, app)
      ];
    } else {
      this.reporters = [setupReporter(config.get('reporter'), stdout, config, app)];

      if (path) {
        if (config.appMode === 'dev') {
          let devModeFileReporter = config.get('dev_mode_file_reporter');
          if (!devModeFileReporter) {
            log.warn('You configured a `report_file`, you may want to configure the `dev_mode_file_reporter` as well. Using the `tap` logger now.');
            devModeFileReporter = 'tap';
          }
          this.reporters.push(setupReporter(devModeFileReporter, this.reportFile.outputStream, config, app));
        } else {
          this.reporters.push(setupReporter(config.get('reporter'), this.reportFile.outputStream, config, app));
        }
      }
    }
  }

  getBailThreshold(config) {
    let bailOnTestFailure = config.get('bail_on_test_failure');

    if (bailOnTestFailure === true) {
      return 1;
    } else if (bailOnTestFailure === false || typeof bailOnTestFailure === 'undefined') {
      return false;
    } else if (typeof bailOnTestFailure === 'number' && isFinite(bailOnTestFailure) && Math.floor(bailOnTestFailure) === bailOnTestFailure && bailOnTestFailure > 0) {
      return bailOnTestFailure;
    } else {
      log.warn('bail_on_test_failure', `Ignoring invalid value: ${bailOnTestFailure}. Defaulting to false.`);
      return false;
    }
  }

  hasBailed() {
    return !!this.bailLauncher;
  }

  getBailReport() {
    return {
      testsRanBeforeBail: this.testsRanBeforeBail,
      bailLauncher: this.bailLauncher,
      failuresByLauncher: this.failuresByLauncher,
      failedTests: this.failedTests
    };
  }

  resetBailState() {
    this.bailReason = null;
    this.testsRanBeforeBail = null;
    this.bailLauncher = null;
    this.failuresByLauncher = {};
    this.failedTests = [];
    this.suppressed = 0;
  }

  isFailure(result) {
    return !result.skipped && !result.todo && !result.passed;
  }

  testStarted(name, data) {
    this.reporters.forEach(reporter => {
      if (reporter.testStarted) {
        reporter.testStarted(name, data);
      }
    });
  }

  close() {
    this.finish();

    if (this.reportFile) {
      return this.reportFile.close();
    }
  }

  hasTests() {
    return this.total > 0;
  }

  hasPassed() {
    return this.total <= ((this.passed || 0) + (this.skipped || 0) + (this.todo || 0));
  }

  report(name, result) {
    if (this.hasBailed()) {
      this.suppressed++;
      return;
    }

    this.total++;
    if (result.skipped) {
      this.skipped++;
    } else if (result.passed && !result.todo) {
      this.passed++;
    } else if (!result.passed && result.todo) {
      this.todo++;
    }

    this.reporters.forEach(reporter => {
      reporter.report(name, result);
    });

    if (this.isFailure(result)) {
      this.failuresByLauncher[name] = (this.failuresByLauncher[name] || 0) + 1;
      this.failedTests.push(result.name);

      if (this.bailThreshold !== false && this.failedTests.length >= this.bailThreshold) {
        this.bailReason = result.name;
        this.bailLauncher = name;
        this.testsRanBeforeBail = this.total;
        this.emit('test-failure', name, result);
      }
    }
  }

  finish() {
    this.reporters.forEach(reporter => {
      if (reporter.bailOut) {
        reporter.bailOut(this.bailReason, this.bailThreshold, this.testsRanBeforeBail, this.suppressed);
      }
      if (reporter.finish) {
        reporter.finish();
      }
    });
  }
}

Reporter.with = (app, stdout, path) => Bluebird.try(() => new Reporter(app, stdout, path)).disposer((reporter, promise) => {
  if (promise.isRejected()) {
    let err = promise.reason();

    if (!err.hideFromReporter) {
      reporter.report(null, {
        passed: false,
        name: err.name || 'unknown error',
        error: {
          message: err.message
        }
      });
    }
  }

  return reporter.close();
});

function forwardToReporters(fn) {
  return function() {
    let args = new Array(arguments.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < args.length; ++i) {
      args[i] = arguments[i];
    }

    this.reporters.forEach(reporter => {
      if (reporter[fn]) {
        reporter[fn].apply(reporter, args);
      }
    });
  };
}

['onStart', 'onEnd', 'reportMetadata'].forEach(fn => {
  Reporter.prototype[fn] = forwardToReporters(fn);
});

module.exports = Reporter;
EOF

cd /app && cat > lib/reporters/tap_reporter.js <<'EOF'
'use strict';

const displayutils = require('../utils/displayutils');

module.exports = class TapReporter {
  constructor(silent, out, config, app) {
    this.out = out || process.stdout;
    this.silent = silent;
    this.app = app;
    this.quietLogs = !!config.get('tap_quiet_logs');
    this.failsOnly = !!config.get('tap_failed_tests_only');
    this.strictSpecCompliance = !!config.get('tap_strict_spec_compliance');
    this.stoppedOnError = null;
    this.id = 1;
    this.total = 0;
    this.pass = 0;
    this.skipped = 0;
    this.todo = 0;
    this.results = [];
    this.errors = [];
    this.logs = [];
    this.logProcessor = config.get('tap_log_processor');

    this.bailReason = null;
    this.bailCount = null;
    this.testsRanBeforeBail = null;
    this.suppressed = null;
  }

  bailOut(reason, count, testsRanBeforeBail, suppressed) {
    this.bailReason = reason;
    this.bailCount = count;
    this.testsRanBeforeBail = testsRanBeforeBail;
    this.suppressed = suppressed;
  }

  report(prefix, data) {
    this.results.push({
      launcher: prefix,
      result: data
    });
    this.display(prefix, data);
    this.total++;

    if (data.skipped) {
      this.skipped++;
    } else if (data.passed && !data.todo) {
      this.pass++;
    } else if (!data.passed && data.todo) {
      this.todo++;
    }
  }

  summaryDisplay() {
    return displayutils.summaryDisplay.call(this);
  }

  /*
   * Based on current settings in this object, will the given value be
   * displayed by 'display'?
   */
  willDisplay(result) {
    let show = !this.silent && !!result && (!this.failsOnly || result.error);
    return show;
  }

  /*
   * Display a formatted message for the result, but only if
   * we've configured to do that.
   */
  display(prefix, result) {
    if (this.willDisplay(result)) {
      this.out.write(displayutils.resultString(this.id++, prefix, result, this.quietLogs, this.strictSpecCompliance, this.logProcessor));
    }
  }

  finish() {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    this.out.write('\n' + this.summaryDisplay() + '\n');

    if (this.bailReason) {
      this.out.write(`Bail out! ${this.bailReason} (${this.bailCount})\n`);
      this.out.write('# bailed\n');
      this.out.write(`# ran before bail ${this.testsRanBeforeBail}\n`);
      this.out.write(`# suppressed ${this.suppressed}\n`);
    }
  }
};

cd /app && cat > lib/reporters/dot_reporter.js <<'EOF'
'use strict';

const indent = require('../utils/strutils').indent;
const printf = require('printf');
const displayutils = require('../utils/displayutils');

module.exports = class DotReporter {
  constructor(silent, out, config, app) {
    this.out = out || process.stdout;
    this.silent = silent;
    this.app = app;
    this.stoppedOnError = null;
    this.id = 1;
    this.total = 0;
    this.pass = 0;
    this.skipped = 0;
    this.todo = 0;
    this.results = [];
    this.startTime = new Date();
    this.endTime = null;
    this.currentLineChars = 0;
    this.maxLineChars = Math.min(this.out.columns || 65, 65) - 5;
    this.out.write('\n');
    this.out.write('  ');

    this.bailReason = null;
    this.bailCount = null;
    this.testsRanBeforeBail = null;
    this.suppressed = null;
  }

  bailOut(reason, count, testsRanBeforeBail, suppressed) {
    this.bailReason = reason;
    this.bailCount = count;
    this.testsRanBeforeBail = testsRanBeforeBail;
    this.suppressed = suppressed;
  }

  report(prefix, data) {
    this.results.push({
      launcher: prefix,
      result: data
    });
    this.display(prefix, data);
    this.total++;
    if (data.skipped) {
      this.skipped++;
    } else if (data.passed && !data.todo) {
      this.pass++;
    } else if (!data.passed && data.todo) {
      this.todo++;
    }
  }

  display(prefix, result) {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    if (this.currentLineChars > this.maxLineChars) {
      this.currentLineChars = 0;
      this.out.write('\n  ');
    }
    if (result.passed && !result.todo) {
      this.out.write('.');
    } else if (!result.passed && result.todo) {
      this.out.write('T');
    } else if (result.skipped) {
      this.out.write('*');
    } else {
      this.out.write('F');
    }
    this.currentLineChars += 1;
  }

  finish() {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    this.endTime = new Date();
    this.out.write('\n\n');
    this.out.write(this.summaryDisplay());
    this.out.write('\n\n');
    this.displayErrors();

    if (this.bailReason) {
      this.out.write(`Bail out! ${this.bailReason} (${this.bailCount})\n`);
      this.out.write('# bailed\n');
      this.out.write(`# ran before bail ${this.testsRanBeforeBail}\n`);
      this.out.write(`# suppressed ${this.suppressed}\n`);
    }
  }

  displayErrors() {
    this.results.forEach((data, idx) => {
      let result = data.result;
      let error = result.error;
      if (!error) {
        return;
      }

      printf(this.out, '%*d) [%s] %s\n', idx + 1, 3, data.launcher, result.name);

      if (error.message) {
        printf(this.out, '     %s\n', error.message);
      }

      if ('expected' in error && 'actual' in error) {
        printf(this.out, '\n' +
               '     expected: %s%O\n' +
               '       actual: %O\n', (error.negative ? 'NOT ' : ''), error.expected, error.actual);
      }

      if (error.stack) {
        printf(this.out, '\n%s', indent(error.stack, 5));
      }

      this.out.write('\n\n');
    }, this);
  }

  summaryDisplay() {
    let lines = [
      `[duration - ${this.duration()} ms]`,
      displayutils.summaryDisplay.call(this),
    ];
    return lines.join('\n');
  }

  duration() {
    return Math.round((this.endTime - this.startTime));
  }
};
EOF

cd /app && cat > lib/reporters/teamcity_reporter.js <<'EOF'
'use strict';

function namify(prefix, result) {
  let line = (prefix ? (prefix + ' - ') : '') +
    result.name.trim();

  return escape(line);
}

/**
 * Borrowed from https://github.com/travisjeffery/mocha-teamcity-reporter
 * Escape the given `str`.
 */

function escape(str) {
  if (!str) {
    return '';
  }
  return str
    .toString()
    .replace(/\|/g, '||')
    .replace(/\n/g, '|n')
    .replace(/\r/g, '|r')
    .replace(/\[/g, '|[')
    .replace(/\]/g, '|]')
    .replace(/\u0085/g, '|x')
    .replace(/\u2028/g, '|l')
    .replace(/\u2029/g, '|p')
    .replace(/'/g, '|\'');
}

function teamcityLine(type, options) {
  const attributes = Object.keys(options)
    .map(attributeName => `${attributeName}='${options[attributeName]}'`)
    .join(' ');

  return `##teamcity[${type} ${attributes}]\n`;
}

function runDurationAttribute(result) {
  return typeof result.runDuration === 'number' ? {duration: result.runDuration} : undefined;
}

module.exports = class TeamcityReporter {
  constructor(silent, out, config, app) {
    this.out = out || process.stdout;
    this.silent = silent;
    this.app = app;
    this.stoppedOnError = null;
    this.id = 1;
    this.total = 0;
    this.pass = 0;
    this.skipped = 0;
    this.startTime = new Date();
    this.endTime = null;

    this.bailReason = null;
    this.bailCount = null;
    this.testsRanBeforeBail = null;
    this.suppressed = null;
  }

  bailOut(reason, count, testsRanBeforeBail, suppressed) {
    this.bailReason = reason;
    this.bailCount = count;
    this.testsRanBeforeBail = testsRanBeforeBail;
    this.suppressed = suppressed;
  }

  report(prefix, data) {
    const name = namify(prefix, data);
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('testStarted', {name}));
    this._display(prefix, data);
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('testFinished', Object.assign(
      {name},
      runDurationAttribute(data)
    )));
    this.total++;
    if (data.skipped) {
      this.skipped++;
    } else if (data.passed) {
      this.pass++;
    }
  }

  finish() {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    this.endTime = new Date();
    if (this.bailReason) {
      this.out.write(teamcityLine('message', {
        text: 'Bail out! ERROR',
        status: 'ERROR',
      }));
      this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildStatisticValue', {
        key: 'bailedTests',
        value: this.bailCount,
      }));
      this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildStatisticValue', {
        key: 'testsBeforeBail',
        value: this.testsRanBeforeBail,
      }));
      this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildStatisticValue', {
        key: 'suppressedAfterBail',
        value: this.suppressed,
      }));
      this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildProblem', {
        description: this.bailReason,
      }));
    }
    this.out.write('\n\n');
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('testSuiteFinished', {
      name: 'testem.suite',
      duration: Math.round((this.endTime - this.startTime)),
    }));
    this.out.write('\n\n');
  }

  _display(prefix, result) {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    const name = namify(prefix, result);

    if (result.skipped) {
      this.out.write(teamcityLine('testIgnored', {
        name,
        message: 'pending',
      }));
    } else if (!result.passed) {
      const hasError = result.error;
      const attributes = {name};

      const message = (hasError && result.error.message) || '';
      const stack = (hasError && result.error.stack) || '';

      attributes.message = escape(message);
      attributes.details = escape(stack);

      if (
        hasError &&
        Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(result.error, 'expected') &&
        Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(result.error, 'actual')
      ) {
        attributes.type = 'comparisonFailure';
        attributes.expected = (result.error.negative ? 'NOT ' : '') + escape(result.error.expected);
        attributes.actual = escape(result.error.actual);
      }

      this.out.write(teamcityLine('testFailed', attributes));
    }
  }
};

module.exports.teamcityLine = teamcityLine;
EOF

cd /app && cat > lib/reporters/xunit_reporter.js <<'EOF'
'use strict';

var XmlDom = require('@xmldom/xmldom');
var indent = require('../utils/strutils').indent;

module.exports = class XUnitReporter {
  constructor(silent, out, config, app) {
    this.out = out || process.stdout;
    this.app = app;
    this.excludeStackTraces = config.get('xunit_exclude_stack');
    this.silent = silent;
    this.stoppedOnError = null;
    this.id = 1;
    this.total = 0;
    this.pass = 0;
    this.skipped = 0;
    this.todo = 0;
    this.results = [];
    this.startTime = new Date();
    this.endTime = null;

    this.bailReason = null;
    this.bailCount = null;
    this.testsRanBeforeBail = null;
    this.suppressed = null;
  }

  bailOut(reason, count, testsRanBeforeBail, suppressed) {
    this.bailReason = reason;
    this.bailCount = count;
    this.testsRanBeforeBail = testsRanBeforeBail;
    this.suppressed = suppressed;
  }

  report(prefix, data) {
    this.results.push({
      launcher: prefix,
      result: data
    });
    this.display();
    this.total++;

    if (data.skipped) {
      this.skipped++;
    } else if (data.passed && !data.todo) {
      this.pass++;
    } else if (!data.passed && data.todo) {
      this.todo++;
    }
  }

  finish() {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    this.endTime = new Date();
    this.out.write(this.summaryDisplay());
    this.out.write('\n');
  }

  summaryDisplay() {
    var doc = new XmlDom.DOMImplementation().createDocument('', 'testsuite');

    var rootNode = doc.documentElement;
    rootNode.setAttribute('name', 'Testem Tests');
    rootNode.setAttribute('tests', `${this.total}`);
    rootNode.setAttribute('skipped', `${this.skipped}`);
    rootNode.setAttribute('todo', `${this.todo}`);
    rootNode.setAttribute('failures', `${this.failures()}`);
    rootNode.setAttribute('timestamp', new Date().toString());
    rootNode.setAttribute('time', `${this.duration() }`);

    if (this.bailReason) {
      rootNode.setAttribute('errors', '1');

      var propertiesNode = doc.createElement('properties');
      propertiesNode.appendChild(this.createPropertyNode(doc, 'bailReason', this.bailReason));
      propertiesNode.appendChild(this.createPropertyNode(doc, 'testsBeforeBail', this.testsRanBeforeBail));
      propertiesNode.appendChild(this.createPropertyNode(doc, 'suppressedAfterBail', this.suppressed));
      rootNode.appendChild(propertiesNode);

      var errorNode = doc.createElement('error');
      errorNode.setAttribute('message', `Bail out! ${this.bailReason} (${this.bailCount})`);
      rootNode.appendChild(errorNode);
    }

    for (var i = 0, len = this.results.length; i < len; i++) {
      var testcaseNode = this.getTestResultNode(doc, this.results[i]);
      rootNode.appendChild(testcaseNode);
    }

    if (this.bailReason) {
      var systemOutNode = doc.createElement('system-out');
      var bailSummary = [
        `Bail out! ${this.bailReason} (${this.bailCount})`,
        '# bailed',
        `# ran before bail ${this.testsRanBeforeBail}`,
        `# suppressed ${this.suppressed}`
      ].join('\n');
      systemOutNode.appendChild(doc.createCDATASection(bailSummary));
      rootNode.appendChild(systemOutNode);
    }

    return doc.documentElement.toString();
  }

  createPropertyNode(doc, name, value) {
    var propertyNode = doc.createElement('property');
    propertyNode.setAttribute('name', name);
    propertyNode.setAttribute('value', value);
    return propertyNode;
  }

  display() {
    // As the output is XML, the XUnitReporter can only write its results after all
    // tests have finished.
    return;
  }

  getTestResultNode(document, result) {
    var launcher = result.launcher;
    result = result.result;

    var resultNode = document.createElement('testcase');
    resultNode.setAttribute('classname', launcher);
    resultNode.setAttribute('name', result.name);
    resultNode.setAttribute('time', this._durationFromMs(result.runDuration));

    var error = result.error;
    if (error) {
      var errorNode = document.createElement('error');
      var errorMessage = '';
      var errorSection = '';

      if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(error, 'actual') &&  Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(error, 'expected')) {
        errorMessage = 'Assertion Failed';

        errorSection += 'Expected:\n';
        errorSection += indent(`${error.expected}`);
        errorSection += '\n\n';

        errorSection += 'Result:\n';
        errorSection += indent((error.negative ? 'NOT ' : '') + error.actual);
        errorSection += '\n\n';
      }

      if (error.stack && !this.excludeStackTraces) {
        errorSection += 'Source:\n';
        errorSection += error.stack;
      }

      if (errorSection) {
        var cdata = document.createCDATASection(errorSection);
        errorNode.appendChild(cdata);
      }

      errorNode.setAttribute('message', error.message || errorMessage);
      resultNode.appendChild(errorNode);
    } else if (result.skipped) {
      var skippedNode = document.createElement('skipped');
      resultNode.appendChild(skippedNode);
    } else if (result.todo) {
      var todoNode = document.createElement('todo');
      resultNode.appendChild(todoNode);
    } else if (!result.passed) {
      var failureNode = document.createElement('failure');
      resultNode.appendChild(failureNode);
    }

    return resultNode;
  }

  failures() {
    return this.total - this.pass - this.skipped - this.todo;
  }

  duration() {
    const endTime = this.endTime ? this.endTime.getTime() : 0;
    const startTime = this.startTime.getTime();

    return this._durationFromMs(endTime - startTime);
  }

  _durationFromMs(ms) {
    if (ms)
    {
      return (ms / 1000).toFixed(3);
    } else
    {
      return 0;
    }
  }
};
EOF
node -e "require('./lib/reporters/xunit_reporter'); console.log('ok')"

cd /app && cat > public/testem/mocha_adapter.js <<'EOF'
/*

mocha_adapter.js
================

Testem`s adapter for Mocha. It works by monkey-patching `Runner.prototype.emit`.

*/

/* globals mocha, emit, Mocha, Testem */
/* globals module */
/* exported mochaAdapter */
'use strict';

function mochaAdapter() {

  var results = {
    failed: 0,
    passed: 0,
    total: 0,
    pending: 0,
    tests: []
  };
  var id = 1;
  var Runner;
  var ended = false;
  var waiting = 0;
  var allTestResultsEmitted = false;

  try {
    Runner = mocha.Runner || Mocha.Runner;
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Testem: failed to register adapter for mocha.');
  }

  function isAborted() {
    return typeof Testem !== 'undefined' && Testem.aborted;
  }

  function getFullName(test) {
    var name = '';
    while (test) {
      name = test.title + ' ' + name;
      test = test.parent;
    }
    return name.replace(/^ /, '');
  }

  /* Store a reference to the global setTimeout function, in case it's
  	 * manipulated by test helpers */
  var _setTimeout = setTimeout;

  var oEmit = Runner.prototype.emit;
  Runner.prototype.emit = function(evt, test, err) {
    var name = getFullName(test);
    if (evt === 'start') {
      if (!isAborted()) {
        emit('tests-start', { name: name });
      }
    } else if (evt === 'end') {
      if (isAborted()) {
        if (!allTestResultsEmitted) {
          allTestResultsEmitted = true;
          emit('all-test-results');
        }
      } else if (waiting === 0) {
        allTestResultsEmitted = true;
        emit('all-test-results');
      }
      ended = true;
    } else if (evt === 'test end') {
      if (!isAborted()) {
        waiting++;
        _setTimeout(function() {
          waiting--;
          if (isAborted()) {
            if (ended && !allTestResultsEmitted) {
              allTestResultsEmitted = true;
              emit('all-test-results');
            }
            return;
          }
          if (test.state === 'passed') {
            testPass(test);
          } else if (test.pending) {
            testPending(test);
          }
          if (ended && waiting === 0 && !allTestResultsEmitted) {
            allTestResultsEmitted = true;
            emit('all-test-results');
          }
        }, 0);
      }
    } else if (evt === 'fail') {
      if (!isAborted()) {
        testFail(test, err);
      }
    }

    oEmit.apply(this, arguments);

    function testPass(test) {
      if (isAborted()) {
        return;
      }
      var tst = {
        passed: 1,
        failed: 0,
        total: 1,
        pending: 0,
        id: id++,
        name: name,
        runDuration: test.duration,
        items: []
      };
      results.passed++;
      results.total++;
      results.tests.push(tst);
      emit('test-result', tst);
    }

    function makeFailingTest(test, err) {
      err = err || test.err;
      var items = [{
        passed: false,
        message: err.message,
        stack: (err && err.stack) ? err.stack : undefined
      }];
      var tst = {
        passed: 0,
        failed: 1,
        total: 1,
        pending: 0,
        id: id++,
        name: name,
        runDuration: test.duration,
        items: items
      };
      return tst;
    }

    function testFail(test, err) {
      if (isAborted()) {
        return;
      }
      var tst = makeFailingTest(test, err);
      results.failed++;
      results.total++;
      results.tests.push(tst);
      emit('test-result', tst);

    }

    function testPending() {
      if (isAborted()) {
        return;
      }
      var tst = {
        passed: 0,
        failed: 0,
        total: 1,
        pending: 1,
        id: id++,
        name: name,
        items: []
      };
      results.total++;
      results.tests.push(tst);
      emit('test-result', tst);
    }
  };

}

// Exporting this as a module so that it can be unit tested in Node.
if (typeof module !== 'undefined') {
  module.exports = mochaAdapter;
}
EOF
node -e "require('./public/testem/mocha_adapter'); console.log('ok')"
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
ok

END STEP

cd /app && cat > public/testem/jasmine2_adapter.js <<'EOF'
/*

 jasmine2_adapter.js
 ==================

 Testem's adapter for Jasmine. It works by adding a custom reporter.

 */

/* globals emit, jasmine, Testem */
/* exported jasmine2Adapter */
'use strict';

function jasmine2Adapter() {

  var results = {
    failed: 0,
    passed: 0,
    total: 0,
    pending: 0,
    tests: []
  };
  var allTestResultsEmitted = false;

  function isAborted() {
    return typeof Testem !== 'undefined' && Testem.aborted;
  }

  function Jasmine2AdapterReporter() {

    this.jasmineStarted = function() {
      if (!isAborted()) {
        emit('tests-start');
      }
    };

    this.specStarted = function(spec) {
      if (isAborted()) {
        return;
      }
      var currentTest = {
        name: spec.fullName
      };
      emit('tests-start', currentTest);
    };

    this.specDone = function(spec) {
      if (isAborted()) {
        return;
      }

      var test = {
        passed: 0,
        failed: 0,
        total: 0,
        pending: 0,
        id: spec.id + 1,
        name: spec.fullName,
        items: []
      };

      var i, l, failedExpectations, item;

      if (spec.status === 'passed') {
        test.passed++;
        test.total++;
        results.passed++;
      } else if (spec.status === 'pending') {
        test.pending++;
        test.total++;
        results.pending++;
      } else {
        failedExpectations = spec.failedExpectations;
        for (i = 0, l = failedExpectations.length; i < l; i++) {
          item = failedExpectations[i];
          test.items.push({
            passed: item.passed,
            message: item.message,
            stack: item.stack || undefined
          });
        }
        test.failed++;
        results.failed++;
        test.total++;
      }

      results.total++;

      emit('test-result', test);
    };

    this.jasmineDone = function() {
      if (isAborted()) {
        if (!allTestResultsEmitted) {
          allTestResultsEmitted = true;
          emit('all-test-results');
        }
        return;
      }
      allTestResultsEmitted = true;
      emit('all-test-results');
    };

  }

  jasmine.getEnv().addReporter(new Jasmine2AdapterReporter());
}
EOF
node -e "require('./public/testem/jasmine2_adapter'); console.log('ok')"
OBSERVATION:

cd /app && cat > public/testem/qunit_adapter.js <<'EOF'
/*

qunit_adapter.js
================

Testem's QUnit adapter. Works by using QUnit's hooks:

* `testStart`
* `testDone`
* `moduleStart`
* `moduleEnd`
* `done`
* `log`

*/

/* globals QUnit, emit, Testem */
/* exported qunitAdapter */
'use strict';

function qunitAdapter() {

  var results = {
    failed: 0,
    passed: 0,
    skipped: 0,
    todo: 0,
    total: 0,
    tests: []
  };
  var currentTest;
  var id = 1;
  var allTestResultsEmitted = false;

  function isAborted() {
    return typeof Testem !== 'undefined' && Testem.aborted;
  }

  function clearQueue() {
    if (QUnit.config && QUnit.config.queue) {
      QUnit.config.queue.length = 0;
    }
  }

  function lineNumber(e) {
    return e.line || e.lineNumber;
  }

  function sourceFile(e) {
    return e.sourceURL || e.fileName;
  }

  function message(e) {
    var msg = (e.name && e.message) ? (e.name + ': ' + e.message) : e.toString();
    return msg;
  }

  function stacktrace(e) {
    if (e.stack) {
      return e.stack;
    }
    return undefined;
  }

  QUnit.log(function(params, e) {
    if (isAborted()) {
      return;
    }
    if (e) {
      currentTest.items.push({
        passed: params.result,
        line: lineNumber(e),
        file: sourceFile(e),
        stack: stacktrace(e) || params.source,
        message: message(e)
      });
    } else {
      if (params.result) {
        currentTest.items.push({
          passed: params.result,
          message: params.message
        });
      } else {
        currentTest.items.push({
          passed: params.result,
          actual: params.actual,
          expected: params.expected,
          stack: params.source,
          message: params.message,
          negative: params.negative
        });
      }

    }

  });
  QUnit.testStart(function(params) {
    if (isAborted()) {
      clearQueue();
      return;
    }
    currentTest = {
      id: id++,
      name: (params.module ? params.module + ': ' : '') + params.name,
      items: []
    };
    emit('tests-start', currentTest);
  });
  QUnit.testDone(function(params) {
    if (isAborted()) {
      clearQueue();
      return;
    }
    currentTest.failed = params.failed;
    currentTest.passed = params.passed;
    currentTest.skipped = params.skipped;
    currentTest.todo = params.todo;
    currentTest.total = params.total;
    currentTest.runDuration = params.runtime;
    currentTest.testId = params.testId;

    results.total++;

    if (currentTest.skipped) {
      results.skipped++;
    } else if (results.failed > 0 && !results.todo) {
      results.failed++;
    } else {
      results.passed++;
    }

    results.tests.push(currentTest);

    emit('test-result', currentTest);
  });
  QUnit.done(function(params) {
    results.runDuration = params.runtime;
    if (isAborted()) {
      clearQueue();
      if (!allTestResultsEmitted) {
        allTestResultsEmitted = true;
        emit('all-test-results');
      }
      return;
    }
    allTestResultsEmitted = true;
    emit('all-test-results');
  });

}
EOF
node -e "require('./public/testem/qunit_adapter'); console.log('ok')"

path = 'public/testem/testem_client.js'
s = open(path).read()

"""  emitMessage: function() {
    if (this._noConnectionRequired) {
      return;

    if (this.aborted) {
      return;
    }

"""  emitMessage: function() {
    if (this._noConnectionRequired) {
      return;
    }
    if (this.aborted && !this._allowAbortMessages) {
      return;
    }
""")

"""  handleAbortTests: function() {
    if (this.aborted) {
      return;
    }
    this.aborted = true;

    var abortMessage = new Message(this, ['abort-tests']);
    var completeMessage = new Message(this, ['after-tests-complete']);

    if (this._isIframeReady) {
      this.emitMessageToIframe(abortMessage);
      this.emitMessageToIframe(completeMessage);
    } else {
      this.enqueueMessage(abortMessage);
      this.enqueueMessage(completeMessage);
    }
  }
""",
"""  handleAbortTests: function() {
    if (this.aborted) {
      return;
    }
    this.aborted = true;
    this._allowAbortMessages = true;
    this.emit('abort-tests');
    this.emit('after-tests-complete');
    this._allowAbortMessages = false;
  }
""")

open(path, 'w').write(s)
print('done')

COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > tests/bail_on_test_failure_tests.js <<'EOF'
'use strict';

const expect = require('chai').expect;
const sinon = require('sinon');
const PassThrough = require('stream').PassThrough;

const Config = require('../lib/config');
const Reporter = require('../lib/utils/reporter');
const TapReporter = require('../lib/reporters/tap_reporter');
const DotReporter = require('../lib/reporters/dot_reporter');
const TeamcityReporter = require('../lib/reporters/teamcity_reporter');
const XUnitReporter = require('../lib/reporters/xunit_reporter');
const FakeReporter = require('./support/fake_reporter');
const App = require('../lib/app');
const Server = require('../lib/server');
const BrowserTestRunner = require('../lib/runners/browser_test_runner');
const ProcessTestRunner = require('../lib/runners/process_test_runner');
const FakeSocket = require('./support/fake_socket');
const Launcher = require('../lib/launcher');
const Testem = require('../public/testem/testem_client');
const Bluebird = require('bluebird');

describe('bail_on_test_failure', function() {
  let sandbox;

  beforeEach(function() {
    sandbox = sinon.createSandbox();
  });

  afterEach(function() {
    sandbox.restore();
  });

  function makeApp(configOptions) {
    configOptions = configOptions || {};
    return {
      config: {
        appMode: 'ci',
        get(key) {
          if (key === 'reporter' && !configOptions.reporter) {
            return FakeReporter;
          }
          return configOptions[key];
        }
      }
    };
  }

  describe('config default', function() {
    it('defaults to false', function() {
      let config = new Config('ci');
      expect(config.get('bail_on_test_failure')).to.equal(false);
    });
  });

  describe('Reporter constructor validation', function() {
    it('accepts true as threshold one', function() {
      let reporter = new Reporter(makeApp({bail_on_test_failure: true}), new PassThrough());
      expect(reporter.bailThreshold).to.equal(1);
    });

    it('accepts a positive integer as threshold', function() {
      let reporter = new Reporter(makeApp({bail_on_test_failure: 3}), new PassThrough());
      expect(reporter.bailThreshold).to.equal(3);
    });

    it('accepts false as disabled', function() {
      let reporter = new Reporter(makeApp({bail_on_test_failure: false}), new PassThrough());
      expect(reporter.bailThreshold).to.equal(false);
    });

    ['zero', 'negative', 'float', 'string'].forEach(function(kind) {
      let value = {zero: 0, negative: -1, float: 1.5, string: '1'}[kind];
      it(`warns and defaults to false for ${kind}`, function() {
        let warn = sandbox.stub(require('npmlog'), 'warn');
        let reporter = new Reporter(makeApp({bail_on_test_failure: value}), new PassThrough());
        expect(warn).to.have.been.calledWith('bail_on_test_failure');
        expect(reporter.bailThreshold).to.equal(false);
      });
    });
  });

  describe('bail behavior', function() {
    let reporter, emitted;

    beforeEach(function() {
      reporter = new Reporter(makeApp({bail_on_test_failure: 2}), new PassThrough());
      emitted = [];
      reporter.on('test-failure', function(name, result) {
        emitted.push({name: name, result: result});
      });
    });

    function result(name, passed) {
      return {name: name, passed: passed};
    }

    it('does not bail on the first failure when threshold is 2', function() {
      reporter.report('launcher1', result('first failure', false));
      expect(reporter.hasBailed()).to.equal(false);
      expect(emitted).to.have.length(0);
      expect(reporter.getBailReport().bailLauncher).to.equal(null);
    });

    it('bails on the Nth failure and emits test-failure', function() {
      reporter.report('launcher1', result('first failure', false));
      reporter.report('launcher2', result('second failure', false));

      expect(reporter.hasBailed()).to.equal(true);
      expect(reporter.bailReason).to.equal('second failure');
      expect(reporter.getBailReport().bailLauncher).to.equal('launcher2');
      expect(reporter.getBailReport().testsRanBeforeBail).to.equal(2);
      expect(reporter.getBailReport().failuresByLauncher).to.deep.equal({launcher1: 1, launcher2: 1});
      expect(reporter.getBailReport().failedTests).to.deep.equal(['first failure', 'second failure']);
      expect(emitted).to.have.length(1);
      expect(emitted[0].name).to.equal('launcher2');
      expect(emitted[0].result.name).to.equal('second failure');
    });

    it('ignores skipped and todo failures', function() {
      reporter.report('launcher1', {name: 'skipped', passed: false, skipped: true});
      reporter.report('launcher1', {name: 'todo', passed: false, todo: true});
      reporter.report('launcher1', result('real failure', false));
      reporter.report('launcher1', result('second real failure', false));

      expect(reporter.hasBailed()).to.equal(true);
      expect(reporter.getBailReport().failedTests).to.deep.equal(['real failure', 'second real failure']);
    });

    it('gates subsequent results from sub-reporters', function() {
      let fake = new FakeReporter();
      let app = makeApp({bail_on_test_failure: 1});
      app.config.get = function(key) {
        if (key === 'reporter') {
          return fake;
        }
        return key === 'bail_on_test_failure' ? 1 : undefined;
      };
      reporter = new Reporter(app, new PassThrough());

      reporter.report('launcher1', result('fails', false));
      reporter.report('launcher1', result('after bail', true));

      expect(fake.results).to.have.length(1);
      expect(fake.results[0].result.name).to.equal('fails');
      expect(reporter.suppressed).to.equal(1);
    });

    it('resetBailState clears bail state and allows reporting again', function() {
      reporter = new Reporter(makeApp({bail_on_test_failure: 1}), new PassThrough());
      reporter.report('launcher1', result('fails', false));
      expect(reporter.hasBailed()).to.equal(true);

      reporter.resetBailState();
      expect(reporter.hasBailed()).to.equal(false);
      expect(reporter.bailReason).to.equal(null);
      expect(reporter.bailLauncher).to.equal(null);
      expect(reporter.getBailReport().failuresByLauncher).to.deep.equal({});
      expect(reporter.getBailReport().failedTests).to.deep.equal([]);

      reporter.report('launcher1', result('fails again', false));
      expect(reporter.hasBailed()).to.equal(true);
      expect(reporter.bailReason).to.equal('fails again');
    });
  });

  describe('reporter output', function() {
    it('tap reporter outputs bail summary', function() {
      let stream = new PassThrough();
      let reporter = new Reporter(makeApp({
        reporter: 'tap',
        bail_on_test_failure: 1,
        tap_quiet_logs: false,
        tap_failed_tests_only: false
      }), stream);
      reporter.report('launcher', {name: 'fails', passed: false});
      reporter.finish();
      let output = stream.read().toString();
      expect(output).to.match(/Bail out! fails \(1\)/);
      expect(output).to.match(/# bailed/);
      expect(output).to.match(/# ran before bail 1/);
      expect(output).to.match(/# suppressed 0/);
    });

    it('dot reporter outputs bail summary', function() {
      let stream = new PassThrough();
      let reporter = new Reporter(makeApp({
        reporter: 'dot',
        bail_on_test_failure: 1
      }), stream);
      reporter.report('launcher', {name: 'fails', passed: false});
      reporter.finish();
      let output = stream.read().toString();
      expect(output).to.match(/Bail out! fails \(1\)/);
      expect(output).to.match(/# bailed/);
      expect(output).to.match(/# ran before bail 1/);
      expect(output).to.match(/# suppressed 0/);
    });

    it('teamcity reporter outputs bail information', function() {
      let stream = new PassThrough();
      let reporter = new Reporter(makeApp({
        reporter: 'teamcity',
        bail_on_test_failure: 1
      }), stream);
      reporter.report('launcher', {name: 'fails', passed: false});
      reporter.finish();
      let output = stream.read().toString();
      expect(output).to.match(/Bail out! ERROR/);
      expect(output).to.match(/buildStatisticValue key='bailedTests' value='1'/);
      expect(output).to.match(/buildStatisticValue key='testsBeforeBail' value='1'/);
      expect(output).to.match(/buildStatisticValue key='suppressedAfterBail' value='0'/);
      expect(output).to.match(/buildProblem/);
    });

    it('xunit reporter outputs bail information', function() {
      let stream = new PassThrough();
      let reporter = new Reporter(makeApp({
        reporter: 'xunit',
        bail_on_test_failure: 1,
        xunit_exclude_stack: false
      }), stream);
      reporter.report('launcher', {name: 'fails', passed: false, runDuration: 1});
      reporter.finish();
      let output = stream.read().toString();
      expect(output).to.match(/errors="1"/);
      expect(output).to.match(/<error message="Bail out! fails \(1\)"/);
      expect(output).to.match(/name="bailReason" value="fails"/);
      expect(output).to.match(/name="testsBeforeBail" value="1"/);
      expect(output).to.match(/name="suppressedAfterBail" value="0"/);
      expect(output).to.match(/<system-out>/);
    });
  });

  describe('App', function() {
    it('getExitCode returns a bail-specific error', function() {
      let app = new App(new Config('ci'));
      app.reporter = {
        hasBailed: function() { return true; },
        getBailReport: function() {
          return {testsRanBeforeBail: 5, bailLauncher: 'launcher'};
        },
        bailReason: 'my failing test',
        hasPassed: function() { return false; },
        hasTests: function() { return true; }
      };
      let err = app.getExitCode();
      expect(err).to.be.an.instanceof(Error);
      expect(err.message).to.match(/my failing test/);
      expect(err.message).to.match(/5/);
      expect(err.message).not.to.match(/Not all tests passed/);
    });

    it('resetBailState resets abort tracking and server broadcast state', function() {
      let app = new App(new Config('ci'));
      app.reporter = new Reporter(makeApp({bail_on_test_failure: 1}), new PassThrough());
      app.reporter.report('launcher', {name: 'fails', passed: false});
      app.abortRunners();

      expect(app.aborted).to.equal(true);
      expect(app.server.abortBroadcasted).to.equal(true);

      app.resetBailState();
      expect(app.aborted).to.equal(false);
      expect(app.server.abortBroadcasted).to.equal(false);
      expect(app.reporter.hasBailed()).to.equal(false);
    });
  });

  describe('Server', function() {
    it('broadcastAbort is idempotent and tolerates uninitialized io', function() {
      let server = new Server(new Config('ci'));
      let emit = sandbox.spy();
      server.broadcastAbort();
      expect(emit).not.to.have.been.called();

      server.io = {emit: emit};
      server.broadcastAbort();
      server.broadcastAbort();
      expect(emit).to.have.been.calledOnce();
      expect(emit).to.have.been.calledWith('abort-tests');

      server.resetAbort();
      server.broadcastAbort();
      expect(emit).to.have.been.calledTwice();
    });
  });

  describe('runners', function() {
    function makeBrowserRunner() {
      let config = new Config('ci');
      let launcher = new Launcher('ci', {id: 1, protocol: 'browser'}, config);
      return new BrowserTestRunner(launcher, new FakeReporter(), null, null, config);
    }

    it('browser runner abort is idempotent and emits abort-tests via socket', function() {
      let runner = makeBrowserRunner();
      let socket = new FakeSocket();
      let emit = sandbox.spy(socket, 'emit');
      runner.tryAttach('browser', 1, socket);

      return runner.abort().then(function() {
        expect(runner.aborted).to.equal(true);
        expect(emit).to.have.been.calledWith('abort-tests');
        return runner.abort();
      }).then(function() {
        expect(emit).to.have.been.calledOnce();
      });
    });

    it('process runner abort is idempotent and suppresses results', function() {
      let config = new Config('ci');
      let launcher = new Launcher('ci', {id: 1, protocol: 'process'}, config);
      let fakeReporter = new FakeReporter();
      let runner = new ProcessTestRunner(launcher, fakeReporter);
      sandbox.stub(runner, 'exit').callsFake(function() { return Bluebird.resolve(); });

      return runner.abort().then(function() {
        return runner.abort();
      }).then(function() {
        expect(runner.aborted).to.equal(true);
        runner.finish(new Error('should be suppressed'));
        expect(fakeReporter.results).to.have.length(0);
      });
    });
  });

  describe('client', function() {
    it('handleAbortTests sets aborted, emits events, and blocks emitMessage', function() {
      Testem.aborted = false;
      Testem._allowAbortMessages = false;
      let emit = sandbox.spy(Testem, 'emit');
      let emitMessage = sandbox.spy(Testem, 'emitMessage');

      Testem.handleAbortTests();

      expect(Testem.aborted).to.equal(true);
      expect(emit).to.have.been.calledWith('abort-tests');
      expect(emit).to.have.been.calledWith('after-tests-complete');

      Testem.emitMessage('test-result', {name: 'x'});
      expect(emitMessage).to.have.been.calledTwice(); // only the two from handleAbortTests
    });
  });
});
EOF
echo 'test file created'

const PassThrough = require('stream').PassThrough;
const Config = require('./lib/config');
const App = require('./lib/app');
const Reporter = require('./lib/utils/reporter');

// Simulate app with reporter
let config = new Config('ci', {}, {bail_on_test_failure: 1, reporter: 'tap'});
let app = new App(config);
let stream = new PassThrough();
app.reporter = new Reporter(app, stream);
app.reporter.on('test-failure', () => app.abortRunners());
app.server.broadcastAbort = function() { console.log('broadcastAbort called'); };
let aborted = [];
app.runners = [{abort: function() { aborted.push('runner'); return Promise.resolve(); }}];

app.reporter.report('launcher', {name: 'fail test', passed: false});
console.log('bailed:', app.reporter.hasBailed());
console.log('aborted:', app.aborted);
console.log('runner aborted:', aborted.length);
app.reporter.finish();
console.log('output:', stream.read().toString());
EOF

STEP 83 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat >> tests/bail_on_test_failure_tests.js <<'EOF'

describe('adapter abort handling', function() {
  let originalTestem;

  beforeEach(function() {
    originalTestem = global.Testem;
    global.Testem = {aborted: false};
  });

  afterEach(function() {
    global.Testem = originalTestem;
  });

  describe('mocha adapter', function() {
    let mochaAdapter, _mocha, _Mocha, Runner, emit, setTimeoutStub;

    beforeEach(function() {
      mochaAdapter = require('../public/testem/mocha_adapter');
      Runner = function() {};
      Runner.prototype.emit = function() {};
      _mocha = {Runner: Runner};
      _Mocha = {Runner: Runner};
      emit = sandbox.spy();
      setTimeoutStub = sandbox.stub();
      sandbox.stub(Runner.prototype, 'emit').callsFake(function() {});
      global.mocha = _mocha;
      global.Mocha = _Mocha;
      global.emit = emit;
      global.setTimeout = setTimeoutStub;
      mochaAdapter();
    });

    afterEach(function() {
      delete global.mocha;
      delete global.Mocha;
      delete global.emit;
      delete global.setTimeout;
    });

    it('suppresses events once aborted and signals all-test-results once', function() {
      let runner = new Runner();
      global.Testem.aborted = true;

      runner.emit('test end', {title: 'foo', state: 'passed'});
      expect(setTimeoutStub).not.to.have.been.called();

      runner.emit('fail', {title: 'foo'}, {message: 'x'});
      expect(emit).not.to.have.been.calledWith('test-result');

      runner.emit('end');
      runner.emit('end');
      expect(emit).to.have.been.calledOnce();
      expect(emit).to.have.been.calledWith('all-test-results');
    });

    it('suppresses inside deferred callbacks after abort', function() {
      let runner = new Runner();
      runner.emit('test end', {title: 'foo', state: 'passed'});
      let deferred = setTimeoutStub.lastCall.args[0];

      global.Testem.aborted = true;
      runner.emit('end');
      deferred();

      expect(emit).not.to.have.been.calledWith('test-result');
      expect(emit).to.have.been.calledOnce();
      expect(emit).to.have.been.calledWith('all-test-results');
    });
  });

  describe('jasmine2 adapter', function() {
    let jasmine2Adapter, addReporter, reporter;

    beforeEach(function() {
      jasmine2Adapter = require('../public/testem/jasmine2_adapter');
      addReporter = sandbox.spy();
      global.jasmine = {
        getEnv: function() {
          return {addReporter: addReporter};
        }
      };
      global.emit = sandbox.spy();
      jasmine2Adapter();
      reporter = addReporter.lastCall.args[0];
    });

    afterEach(function() {
      delete global.jasmine;
      delete global.emit;
    });

    it('suppresses events once aborted and signals all-test-results once', function() {
      global.Testem.aborted = true;
      reporter.jasmineStarted();
      reporter.specStarted({fullName: 'foo'});
      reporter.specDone({id: 0, fullName: 'foo', status: 'failed', failedExpectations: []});
      reporter.jasmineDone();
      reporter.jasmineDone();

      expect(global.emit).to.have.been.calledOnce();
      expect(global.emit).to.have.been.calledWith('all-test-results');
    });
  });

  describe('qunit adapter', function() {
    let qunitAdapter, hooks;

    beforeEach(function() {
      qunitAdapter = require('../public/testem/qunit_adapter');
      hooks = {};
      global.QUnit = {
        config: {queue: ['one', 'two']},
        log: function(fn) { hooks.log = fn; },
        testStart: function(fn) { hooks.testStart = fn; },
        testDone: function(fn) { hooks.testDone = fn; },
        done: function(fn) { hooks.done = fn; }
      };
      global.emit = sandbox.spy();
      qunitAdapter();
    });

    afterEach(function() {
      delete global.QUnit;
      delete global.emit;
    });

    it('suppresses events once aborted, clears the queue, and signals all-test-results once', function() {
      global.Testem.aborted = true;
      hooks.testStart({module: 'm', name: 't'});
      hooks.testDone({failed: 1, passed: 0, skipped: 0, todo: 0, total: 1, runtime: 1});
      hooks.done({runtime: 1});
      hooks.done({runtime: 1});

      expect(global.QUnit.config.queue).to.have.length(0);
      expect(global.emit).to.have.been.calledOnce();
      expect(global.emit).to.have.been.calledWith('all-test-results');
    });
  });
});

    return !!this.bailLauncher;
  }
""",
"""  hasBailed() {
    return this.bailReason !== null;
  }
""")
open(path, 'w').write(s)
print('done')

const PassThrough = require('stream').PassThrough;
const Config = require('./lib/config');
const App = require('./lib/app');
const Reporter = require('./lib/utils/reporter');

let config = new Config('ci', {}, {reporter: 'tap', bail_on_test_failure: 1});
let app = new App(config, () => {});
let stream = new PassThrough();
app.reporter = new Reporter(app, stream);
// Manually wire like start() does
app.reporter.on('test-failure', () => app.abortRunners());
let broadcasted = [];
app.server.broadcastAbort = function() { broadcasted.push('abort'); };
let aborted = [];
app.runners = [{abort: function() { aborted.push('x'); return Promise.resolve(); }}];

app.reporter.report('launcher', {name: 'fail', passed: false});
console.log('broadcast:', broadcasted.length, 'aborted:', aborted.length, 'app.aborted:', app.aborted);
EOF
